🤖 RAG ANSWER (59.6s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To apply for the Erasmus internship program, you must meet the minimum GPA
   requirement (2.20 for undergraduates or 2.50 for graduate students) and
   obtain an acceptance letter from the host organization for at least 2
   months. Submit your CV, transcript, and acceptance letter to the
   Internship Office by the announced deadline (starting end of October).
   Applications are ranked based on Erasmus+ scores, and approved students
   receive grant allocations sequentially. After selection, complete the
   Erasmus+ Internship Mobility Agreement, Learning Agreement, Grant
   Agreement, and required travel/insurance documents before starting the
   internship.
------------------------------------------------------------

   destek miktarı × hareketlilik gün sayısı + seyahat katkı payı formülüyle

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (66.4s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Learning Agreement (LA) process for exchange students involves several
   steps. Students prepare the LA with their exchange academic advisor,
   specifying courses to be taken abroad, SU equivalent credits, course
   types, and ECTS values, and submit it within the announced period. The LA
   must be signed by both the student and the partner institution. Students
   are responsible for informing the advisor of any course changes and
   obtaining approval before completing the term abroad. After approval,
   students print, sign, and submit the LA to the host institution for
   signatures, then submit the signed LA to the International Relations
   Office (IRO). The IRO forwards the LA to the Faculty Administrative
   Officer for final approval. Graduate students may also prepare an LA for
   research/projects during mobility.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (66.4s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Here’s how the disciplinary objection process works at Sabancı University:
   1. **Objection Submission**: Students can object to disciplinary sanctions
   imposed by supervisors or committees within **15 days** by submitting a
   written appeal to the **University Administrative Board**.   2. **Review
   Process**: The Board examines the objection and must conclude its decision
   definitively within **15 days**. It may **accept, reject, or modify** the
   sanction.   3. **Further Appeals**: If rejected, the disciplinary board or
   supervisor finalizes the objection based on the grounds for rejection.
   Students retain the right to escalate to **administrative jurisdiction**
   without exhausting the internal objection process.   4. **Notifications**:
   For suspended/expelled students, notifications to relevant units are
   delayed until the objection period (15 days) ends or, if an objection is
   filed, within **3 days** after the result is concluded.   5. **Housing-
   Specific**: For dormitory-related sanctions (e.g., removal), objections
   must be filed within **5 working days**, and the Disciplinary Board
   decides within **7 working days**.   Penalties like suspension exclude the
   period from maximum study duration, and tuition obligations remain for
   suspended students. Scholarship impacts are evaluated separately under
   financial aid rules.   *(Note: All timelines and procedures are fixed; no
   variations exist for undergraduate/graduate students in the provided
   context.)*
------------------------------------------------------------

   Disiplin suçlarında zamanaşımı süreleri şu şekildedir:   1. **Genel
   Süre**: Disiplin cezası gerektiren fiilin işlendiği tarihten itibaren en
   geç **2 yıl** içinde ceza verilmediğinde zamanaşımına uğrar. Ancak adli
   yargı hükmü gerekiyorsa, bu süre kesinleşme tarihinden başlar.   2.
   **Soruşturma Başlama Süreleri**:   - **Uyarma, kınama, 1 hafta-1 ay
   uzaklaştırma** cezaları için: Fiilin öğrenildiği tarihten itibaren **1
   ay** içinde soruşturma başlamalıdır.   - **1-2 yarıyıl uzaklaştırma veya
   çıkarma** cezaları için: **3 ay** içinde soruşturma başlamalıdır.   3.
   **Cinsel Taciz Özel Durumu**:   - Öğrenciler için cinsel taciz/saldırı
   suçlarında soruşturma, fiilin işlendiği tarihten itibaren **en geç 2 yıl**
   içinde tamamlanmalıdır.   4. **Karar Süreleri**:   - Uyarma, kınama gibi
   hafif cezalar için soruşturma tamamlandıktan sonra **10 gün** içinde karar
   verilir.   - Ağır cezalar (çıkarma gibi) için Disiplin Kurulu, dosyayı
   aldıktan sonra **10 gün** içinde karar vermelidir.   *Not*: Adli yargı
   süreci veya özel durumlarda (örneğin cinsel taciz) farklı süreler
   uygulanabilir.

   dönem uzatılabilir.  2. **Lisansüstü Öğrencileri**: Yüksek lisans
   öğrencileri için burs ödemeleri en fazla 10 ay (Eylül-Haziran), doktora
   öğrencileri için ise 12 ay (Eylül-Ağustos) süreyle yapılır. Burs
   devamlılığı her akademik dönem başında değerlendirilir ve Enstitü
   tarafından belirlenen kriterlere göre karara bağlanır.  Ayrıca,
   öğrencilerin ders kayıtlarını tamamlamaları ve akademik performanslarını
   sürdürmeleri burs devamı için gereklidir.
------------------------------------------------------------

   notlar GNO hesaplamasından çıkarılır ve sadece en son alınan not GNO'ya
   dahil edilir. Ancak, öğrencinin kayıt yaptırdığı tüm dersler transkriptte
   gösterilir. Notu B veya B’den yüksek olan dersler tekrarlanamaz.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (99.6s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To open a graduate program, an application must be submitted to YOK with
   the recommendation of the relevant Graduate School Board, the opinion of
   the UEC under the main coordination of the VP-E, the AC decision, and the
   approval of the Board of Trustees. Education begins once YOK approves the
   program. For closing a graduate program, the Graduate School Board, under
   the main coordination of the VP-E, obtains the UEC's opinion and submits
   justifications for AC approval, which is then presented to the Board of
   Trustees. The final decision is notified to YOK, and information about the
   closed program is stored to preserve student history. Changes to existing
   graduate programs, such as course requirements or credits, follow specific
   procedures for course modifications.

   dönemde (mezuniyet aşamasında) olmamak, 8) Hazırlık sınıfı öğrencileri

   derslerin başlamasını izleyen 2. veya 3. hafta içinde olabilir. İsteğe
   bağlı yaz okulunda ise derslerin başladığı ilk hafta içinde ders ekleme-
   bırakma işlemleri yapılır.

🔗 RELATIONS (From Knowledge Graph):

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (69.8s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   At Sabancı University, GPA is calculated by multiplying each course's SU
   credit by its grade coefficient, then dividing the total points by the
   total SU credits of all courses taken in that term. For undergraduate
   students, the calculation includes all courses taken, and the last grade
   received for a repeated course is used. Graduate students follow the same
   method, but the calculation is based on courses taken in the related term.
   Both undergraduate and graduate GPAs are displayed with two decimal
   places.
------------------------------------------------------------

   ödemesi gerekir (bir dönem izin için sadece o dönemin ücreti).  2. İzin
   süresince aylık burs ödemeleri askıya alınır ve bu dönem burs süresinden
   düşülür.   Lisansüstü öğrenciler için ek olarak:  - Askerlik tehir
   işlemleri izin döneminde askıya alınmaz, ancak izin bitiminde kayıt
   yenilemezse tehir iptal edilir.  - Kayıt dondurma durumunda ikamet izni
   otomatik iptal edilir.   Yurt kullanıcıları için ilişik kesme işlemi 2 gün
   içinde tamamlanmalıdır.

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (54.5s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   For students, a leave of absence can be requested due to health,
   financial, family, personal, academic reasons, or unexpected situations,
   documented with proof. Applications must be submitted by the last business
   day of the 7th week following the start of classes. If approved, students
   pay 50% of the semester fee for a semester leave or 50% of the annual
   tuition fee for a full academic year leave. For military service, arrest,
   or custody situations, students must apply within 2 months of the event,
   with approval from the relevant Faculty or Graduate School Administrative
   Board. If students return early from leave, the paid leave fee is deducted
   from the tuition of the registered semester.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (55.7s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The disciplinary penalties for students at Sabancı University include:  1.
   **Warning** and **reprimand** for minor offenses.  2. **Suspension** from
   the university for periods ranging from **one week to one month** for
   moderate offenses.  3. **Suspension for one or two semesters** or
   **dismissal from the university** for severe offenses.  4. **Removal from
   dormitories** may apply for housing-related violations, decided by the
   Disciplinary Board.   Repeat offenses result in a **one-grade heavier
   penalty**, but dismissal is not imposed for repetition alone. Suspended
   students cannot enter campus and must still pay tuition for the suspended
   semester.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (66.5s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The graduation audit process begins with students applying for graduation
   through the SIS within the dates specified in the academic calendar.
   Students must track their courses, SU/ECTS credits, and remaining
   requirements via SIS. The Student Resources (SR) and Faculties/Institutes
   must reach full agreement on the graduation audit and follow the specified
   procedures. If a student does not qualify for graduation at the end of the
   semester, they must renew their application for the next semester. The
   Institutes submit the Graduation Lists to the SR, approved by the Director
   of Graduate School, confirming the completion of graduation requirements.
   Diplomas are issued to eligible students who have completed all necessary
   procedures.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (67.0s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   If you find a lost item on campus, report it by calling extension 3565 or
   bring it to the Security Office in Building D2. Security officers will
   prepare a two-copy Found Item Return Form, which both the finder and the
   officer will sign. The original form stays with the Security Unit, while
   the finder receives the second copy. Found items are then numbered and
   categorized as valuable or non-valuable, placed in separate labeled bags,
   and stored for up to one year. If you lose an item, report it verbally and
   in writing to the Security Unit and, if applicable, to the IT Unit or
   local authorities.

   **Personal Research Fund (PRF)**. The President’s Office Research Fund is

🤖 RAG ANSWER (69.2s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Special event and catering services must be arranged by submitting the
   "Special Event and Catering Request Form" on SUForm at least **30 days**
   before the event. The requesting unit cannot make advance procurements for
   future dates. The Food and Beverage Services (FBS) unit evaluates and
   approves catering companies every **3 years** for hygiene, service, and
   equipment suitability. FBS prepares the event menu and specifications upon
   request and coordinates with Procurement and Operations (POP) for supplier
   selection. The requesting unit is responsible for any disruptions caused
   by late submissions. Catering services can include meals, coffee breaks,
   and cocktails.

   Sabancı University provides several health services on campus for
   students, including:  1. **Health insurance coverage** for all students
   (except Exchange and Project Management Undergraduate students), valid
   from school opening until graduation, excluding coverage abroad.  2.
   Access to **private health services** from contracted institutions if
   referred by the Health Center Physician.  3. **Psychiatry and psychologist
   examinations** available by appointment via phone (9923) or email
   (healthcenter@sabanciuniv.edu).  4. **Emergency health services** provided
   by a fully equipped ambulance team (doctor, anesthesia technician, and
   driver).  5. **Preventive health services**, such as blood tests (complete
   blood count, Hepatitis A/B, MMR) and chest X-ray reports (signed by a
   radiologist or pulmonologist) during enrollment.  6. Inpatient treatments
   (e.g., surgery) do not require a referral. Foreign students have a 40%
   participation rate in health insurance.

   Araştırma Etik Kurulu (AEK) başvuruları, proje/araştırma başlangıç
   tarihinden önce ve ilgili fakültenin AEK değerlendirme periyoduna göre

   hazırlanmalı ve gönüllülük esasına dayalı, onam formu mevcut, gizlilik
   esaslarının belirtildiği gibi gereklilikleri karşılamalıdır.
   Başvuruların değerlendirilmesinde, gerektiğinde kurul dışından kişilerin
   görüşüne başvurulabilir ve bu kişiler toplantıya davet edilebilir veya
   yazılı görüşleri alınabilir. Uygun şekilde hazırlanmış ve temel kuralları
   karşılayan başvurular, AEK'nın toplanmasına gerek kalmadan Araştırma
   Projeleri Geliştirme Ofisi (PGO) tarafından onaylanır.  Başvuru sürecinde
   AEK'nın ulusal veya uluslararası başvuru formlarının doldurulması
   gereklidir. Başvuru sonrasında AEK kararları, proje yürütücüsüne
   elektronik ortamda bildirilir.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (89.1s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The requirements for the Rectorate Research Fund include:  1. Full-time
   faculty members must have started their activities at Sabancı University
   within the last two years and completed at least six months at the
   institution.  2. Faculty members must have made at least two documented
   external grant project applications or provided one guaranteed external
   financing by the end of the project.  3. Faculty members must have
   published high-impact scientific articles.  4. The proposed project must
   be compatible with the faculty member’s other initial or ongoing research
   activities.  5. Faculty members can apply to this fund only once.  6. The
   project duration must not exceed 36 months, with a maximum budget of
   300,000 TL for theoretical research and 600,000 TL for experimental
   research.
------------------------------------------------------------
